\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{convertmol\_c()}{convertmol\_c()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespaceconvertmod_ac48eb19b92dd8e3ed4685cd6e9aec8ae} 
subroutine convertmod\+::convertmol\+\_\+c}



Converts molecular orbitals between different representations. 

This subroutine handles the conversion of molecular orbital coefficients, particularly after optimization steps. It manages complex and real representations, handles phase factors, and computes overlap matrices.


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{in,out}}  & {\em detmat\+\_\+c} & Complex determinant matrix to be modified \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em ipc} & Complex parameter (1 or 2) controlling representation \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em ipf} & Phase factor parameter \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em opposite\+\_\+phase} & Flag for opposite phase handling \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em same\+\_\+phase} & Flag for same phase handling \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em real\+\_\+contracted} & Flag for real contracted basis \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em symmagp} & Symmetry magnetic parameter \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\Hypertarget{namespaceconvertmod_ae41508be7012ab3535ff6fd696e5a7fd}\index{convertmod@{convertmod}!convertmol\_fast@{convertmol\_fast}}
\index{convertmol\_fast@{convertmol\_fast}!convertmod@{convertmod}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{convertmol\_fast()}{convertmol\_fast()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespaceconvertmod_ae41508be7012ab3535ff6fd696e5a7fd} 
subroutine convertmod\+::convertmol\+\_\+fast}



Converts molecular orbitals in the wavefunction. 

This subroutine appends or replaces molecular orbitals at the end of fort.\+10 (the wavefunction in Turbo\+RVB notation). It handles coefficients of both molecular and atomic contracted orbitals.


\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em molopt} & Main control parameter\+:
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item molopt=+/-\/1\+: Ignore coefficients of contracted orbitals
\item molopt=+/-\/2\+: Evaluate AGP coefficients with DMRG
\item molopt=+/-\/3\+: Evaluate both AGP and J coefficients with DMRG
\item molopt \texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}= 2\+: Do not optimize coefficients
\item molopt \texorpdfstring{$<$}{<} 2\+: Always optimize contracted orbital coefficients 
\end{DoxyItemize}\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{evalovers()}{evalovers()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespaceconvertmod_a7040b9e644d66815742e601a57ea70bf} 
subroutine convertmod\+::evalovers}



Evaluates overlap matrices between orbitals. 

This subroutine calculates overlap matrices between different orbitals. It handles mesh-\/based calculations and uses buffer arrays for efficient computation.


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em overs} & Overlap matrix between orbitals \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em buffer} & Temporary buffer array for calculations \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em mesh} & Total number of mesh points (nx \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} ny \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} nz) \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em volmesh} & Volume element for integration \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\Hypertarget{namespaceconvertmod_a9d9376d6c01cbf3ad11d7729bc3eaa40}\index{convertmod@{convertmod}!ortho\_fast@{ortho\_fast}}
\index{ortho\_fast@{ortho\_fast}!convertmod@{convertmod}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ortho\_fast()}{ortho\_fast()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespaceconvertmod_a9d9376d6c01cbf3ad11d7729bc3eaa40} 
subroutine convertmod\+::ortho\+\_\+fast}



Fast orthogonalization routine for molecular orbitals. 

This subroutine performs fast orthogonalization of molecular orbitals. It handles mesh calculations, volume calculations, and allocates buffers for overlap matrices and molecular orbital coefficients.

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Uses MPI for parallel execution when compiled with PARALLEL flag 
\end{DoxyNote}

Projects derivatives for molecular orbital calculations. 

This subroutine handles projection of derivatives in molecular orbital calculations, with special handling for symmetric AGP cases.


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nelorbh} & Number of half orbitals \\

Projects matrices for molecular orbital calculations. 

This subroutine performs matrix projections for molecular orbital calculations. It handles both symmetric and non-\/symmetric cases for real and complex wavefunctions.


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nelorbh} & Number of orbitals per spin channel \\

\hline

\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nmol} & Number of molecular orbitals \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in,out}}  & {\em projmat} & Projection matrix (dimension\+: ipc\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}nelorbh x nmolmat x \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}) \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nmolmat} & Dimension of molecular matrices \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in,out}}  & {\em dermat} & Derivative matrix (dimension\+: ipc\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}nelorbh x \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}) \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em psip} & Temporary work array \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em symmagp} & Logical flag for symmetric AGP \\
\hline

\Hypertarget{namespaceconvertmod_a465cb68e5bcbb49ad5171567c8605d41}\index{convertmod@{convertmod}!shift\_originref@{shift\_originref}}
\index{shift\_originref@{shift\_originref}!convertmod@{convertmod}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{shift\_originref()}{shift\_originref()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespaceconvertmod_a465cb68e5bcbb49ad5171567c8605d41} 
subroutine convertmod\+::shift\+\_\+originref}



Shifts the reference origin for coordinate system alignment. 

This subroutine calculates and sets the reference origin for the coordinate system. For non-\/periodic systems, it uses the average ion position as reference and applies grid offsets. For periodic systems, it handles coordinate transformations between Cartesian and crystal coordinates. The reference origin is shifted based on mesh parameters and crystal lattice vectors. \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
For parallel runs, the reference origin is broadcast to maintain consistency across processors 
\end{DoxyNote}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{update\_dup\_c()}{update\_dup\_c()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespaceconvertmod_a6910f93af6e52bf9529c57c8eb34ab1d} 
subroutine convertmod\+::update\+\_\+dup\+\_\+c}



Updates dup\+\_\+c matrix with molecular orbital coefficients. 

This subroutine fills the dup\+\_\+c matrix with molecular orbital coefficients from the molecorb matrix for both real and complex wavefunctions. It handles unpaired molecular orbitals and EAGP Pfaffian calculations.


\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Processes both real and complex wavefunctions
\item Handles unpaired molecular orbitals via molecorb\+\_\+unpaired array
\item Updates eigenvalues via eigmolu array 
\end{DoxyItemize}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{upmuctranspip()}{upmuctranspip()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespaceconvertmod_a9c8c82ceef749b80e9da7acdfb71e64f} 
subroutine convertmod\+::upmuctranspip}



Updates mu\+\_\+c matrix and transpip arrays. 

This subroutine handles updating the mu\+\_\+c matrix while preserving transpip and multranspip arrays in the case of complex wavefunctions. The mu\+\_\+c matrix is doubled instead of modifying the transpip arrays.


\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Allocates temporary mu\+\_\+sav array to store matrix values
\item Handles complex wavefunctions by doubling mu\+\_\+c rather than modifying transpip
\item Used for updating molecular orbital coefficients 
\end{DoxyItemize}
